Commit 440ee970 (padre ae0f25ed), árbol fbb6144563761ef0c02b903ac8b324151aaa887c. Config efectiva: TWIN_COST_FIRST=1.95 TWIN_COST_FIJO=1 TWIN_TOF_SHORT_K=0.5 TWIN_MOTOR_CURVA_REAL=1 TWIN_MOTOR_REAL=1 TWIN_CAMERA_FROM_CALIB=1 TWIN_VBAT=11.5 TWIN_LENS_CALIB=pure_pursuit/lens_calib.npz TWIN_FW_OVER='{}' LAT_OUT=/tmp/lat, uno.sh <tag> 4.28 5.8 <ruido> <semilla>, TWIN_PI_EXTRA='{"ROUTE_SEAT_POSTERIOR":true}' ROUTE_SEAT_POSTERIOR_LOG=1.
